My thoughts feel… off sometimes, but not strange like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you had a period of at least two weeks where you felt sad or empty most of the time since you started feeling slowed down?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think I’ve had a clear two-week stretch where I’d say I was sad or empty most of the time. It’s more just been this slowed-down, stuck feeling.\n\nDoctor: Did you have a period where you felt unstoppable and excited before you started feeling slowed down?\n\nPatient: Yeah. After I got fired, I had this stretch where I felt kind of unstoppable, like I was really excited and sure I could do anything. But if someone questioned what I was doing or told me to slow down, I’d get really angry, like they were trying to mess everything up for me.\n\nDoctor: How long did this unstoppable and excited feeling last after you got fired?\n\nPatient: About four months, I think. It started after I got fired from the restaurant and kind of kept going from there.\n\nDoctor: Did your unstoppable and excited feeling after getting fired cause problems in your relationships or daily life?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it caused a lot of problems. I couldn’t really keep work plans straight, or figure out rent stuff, or follow through on things I told my family I’d do. It got to the point where my sister had to take me to a crisis clinic because I just wasn’t able to get basic responsibilities organized on my own.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
